== density + hwacha (AC-04, AC-05) ==
  -- AC-04 density counts known coordinates, boundary inclusive
     PASS  8 enemies placed at known coordinates (= 8)
     PASS  count = 3 inside + 2 exactly on the radius (= 5)
     PASS  batch evaluate matches the single-zone count (= 5)
  -- AC-04 dead enemies leave the density immediately
     PASS  all six counted while alive (= 6)
     PASS  one lethal volley kills all six (= 6)
     PASS  density drops to zero, no dead enemy counted (= 0)
     PASS  each death counted exactly once (= 6)
     PASS  alive count consistent (= 0)
  -- AC-05 hwacha aims at the densest in-range zone and ignores out-of-range
     PASS  picks the densest zone inside the firing range (= 1)
     PASS  follows the density when it moves to another in-range zone (= 0)
     PASS  a zone exactly at the range boundary is targetable (= 0)
     PASS  a zone just beyond the range is not (= -1)
  -- AC-05 equal density ties break on the stable zone id
     PASS  three-way tie picks the lowest zone id (= 0)
     PASS  tie among the leaders picks the lowest of those ids (= 1)
     PASS  tie-break is stable across repeated evaluations
  -- AC-05 no enemies means no shot, and the cooldown is not burned
     PASS  an empty zone is never targeted (= -1)
     PASS  an empty field produces no shots (= 0)
     PASS  hwacha #1 stays ready while holding fire (= 0.0)
     PASS  hwacha #2 stays ready while holding fire (= 0.0)
     PASS  hwacha #3 stays ready while holding fire (= 0.0)
     PASS  hwacha #4 stays ready while holding fire (= 0.0)
     PASS  fires on the first step after a target appears (= 1)
  -- AC-05 a volley damages each enemy once and counts each death once
     PASS  7 enemies staged (= 7)
     PASS  first volley kills nobody (= 0)
     PASS  enemy exactly on the blast edge took damage once (= 35.0)
     PASS  enemy just outside took no damage (= 60.0)
     PASS  distant enemy took no damage (= 60.0)
     PASS  second volley still kills nobody (= 0)
     PASS  damage accumulates once per volley (= 10.0)
     PASS  third volley kills the five inside the blast (= 5)
     PASS  kill counter totals five, no double counting (= 5)
     PASS  the two outside survive (= 2)
     PASS  firing again over the dead adds no kills (= 0)
     PASS  kill counter unchanged (= 5)
  -- AC-05 cooldown gates the fire rate
     PASS  5 s at a 1 s cooldown produced 5 volleys
